  │ 344 + def record_exhaustion_attempt(                                   │

  │ 396 +     """Authorize a force operation under just war criteria.      │
  │ 397 +                                                                  │
  │ 398 +     This is the main entry point for ethical warfare verificati… │
  │ 399 +     All four just war criteria must be satisfied.                │
  │ 400 +                                                                  │
  │ 401 +     Args:                                                        │
  │ 402 +         state: Crusader bridge state                             │
  │ 403 +         holder_id: Capability holder                             │
  │ 404 +         cap: Crusader capability                                 │
  │ 405 +         operation: Type of force operation                       │
  │ 406 +         target_id: Target of operation                           │
  │ 407 +         force_level: Requested force level (0-1)                 │
  │ 408 +         affected_resources: Resources to be affected             │
  │ 409 +         threat_level: Level of threat being responded to         │
  │ 410 +         cause_documentation: Documentation of just cause         │
  │ 411 +         timestamp: Operation timestamp                           │
  │ 412 +                                                                  │
  │ 413 +     Returns:                                                     │
  │ 414 +         (new_state, status, record, proof)                       │
  │ 415 +         record is None if authorization denied                   │
  │ 416 +     """                                                          │
  │ 417 +     import hashlib                                               │
  │ 418 +                                                                  │
  │ 419 +     # Verify capability held                                     │
  │ 420 +     holder_caps = state.get_caps(holder_id)                      │
  │ 421 +     if cap not in holder_caps:                                   │
  │ 422 +         status = EthicalStatus.DENIED_AUTHORITY                  │
  │ 423 +         return state, status, None, ProofObject(                 │
  │ 424 +             rule="AuthorizeForceOperation",                      │
  │ 425 +             premises=[f"holder={holder_id}", "cap not held"],    │
  │ 426 +             conclusion="DENIED: invalid capability"              │
  │ 427 +         )                                                        │
  │ 428 +                                                                  │
  │ 429 +     # Verify all four just war criteria                          │
  │ 430 +     checks = []                                                  │
  │ 431 +                                                                  │
  │ 432 +     # 1. Just Cause                                              │
  │ 433 +     just_cause_ok, just_cause_proof = verify_just_cause(         │
  │ 434 +         state, cap, operation, target_id, cause_documentation    │
  │ 435 +     )                                                            │
  │ 436 +     checks.append(("just_cause", just_cause_ok, just_cause_proo… │
  │ 437 +                                                                  │
  │ 438 +     # 2. Legitimate Authority                                    │
  │ 439 +     authority_ok, authority_proof = verify_legitimate_authority… │
  │ 440 +     checks.append(("authority", authority_ok, authority_proof))  │
  │ 441 +                                                                  │
  │ 442 +     # 3. Proportionality                                         │
  │ 443 +     proportionality_ok, proportionality_proof = verify_proporti… │
  │ 444 +         cap, operation, force_level, affected_resources, threat… │
  │ 445 +     )                                                            │
  │ 446 +     checks.append(("proportionality", proportionality_ok, propo… │
  │ 447 +                                                                  │
  │ 448 +     # 4. Necessity                                               │
  │ 449 +     necessity_ok, necessity_proof = verify_necessity(state, cap… │
  │ 450 +     checks.append(("necessity", necessity_ok, necessity_proof))  │
  │ 451 +                                                                  │
  │ 452 +     # Determine overall status                                   │
  │ 453 +     all_passed = all(ok for _, ok, _ in checks)                  │
  │ 454 +                                                                  │
  │ 455 +     if all_passed:                                               │
  │ 456 +         status = EthicalStatus.AUTHORIZED                        │
  │ 457 +     else:                                                        │
  │ 458 +         # Find which check failed                                │
  │ 459 +         failed = [name for name, ok, _ in checks if not ok]      │
  │ 460 +         if "just_cause" in failed:                               │
  │ 461 +             status = EthicalStatus.DENIED_JUST_CAUSE             │
  │ 462 +         elif "authority" in failed:                              │
  │ 463 +             status = EthicalStatus.DENIED_AUTHORITY              │
  │ 464 +         elif "proportionality" in failed:                        │
  │ 465 +             status = EthicalStatus.DENIED_PROPORTIONALITY        │
  │ 466 +         else:                                                    │
  │ 467 +             status = EthicalStatus.DENIED_NECESSITY              │
  │ 468 +                                                                  │
  │ 469 +     # Create operation record                                    │
  │ 470 +     operation_id = hashlib.sha256(                               │
  │ 471 +         f"{holder_id}:{target_id}:{operation.name}:{timestamp}"… │
  │ 472 +     ).hexdigest()[:16]                                           │
  │ 473 +                                                                  │
  │ 474 +     # Combine proofs                                             │
  │ 475 +     proof_data = "|".join(f"{name}:{proof.proof_hash}" for name… │
  │ 476 +     combined_proof = ProofObject(                                │
  │ 477 +         rule="AuthorizeForceOperation",                          │
  │ 478 +         premises=[                                               │
  │ 479 +             f"operation={operation.name}",                       │
  │ 480 +             f"target={target_id}",                               │
  │ 481 +             f"force={force_level}",                              │
  │ 482 +             f"checks={len(checks)}"                              │
  │ 483 +         ],                                                       │
  │ 484 +         conclusion=f"status={status.name}"                       │
  │ 485 +     )                                                            │
  │ 486 +                                                                  │
  │ 487 +     record = None                                                │
  │ 488 +     if status == EthicalStatus.AUTHORIZED:                       │
  │ 489 +         record = ForceOperationRecord(                           │
  │ 490 +             operation_id=operation_id,                           │
  │ 491 +             operation_type=operation,                            │
  │ 492 +             target_id=target_id,                                 │
  │ 493 +             initiator_id=holder_id,                              │
  │ 494 +             crusader_cap=cap,                                    │
  │ 495 +             ethical_status=status,                               │
  │ 496 +             just_cause_verified=just_cause_ok,                   │
  │ 497 +             authority_verified=authority_ok,                     │
  │ 498 +             proportionality_verified=proportionality_ok,         │
  │ 499 +             necessity_verified=necessity_ok,                     │
  │ 500 +             force_level=force_level,                             │
  │ 501 +             affected_resources=affected_resources,               │
  │ 502 +             timestamp=timestamp,                                 │
  │ 503 +             proof_hash=combined_proof.proof_hash                 │
  │ 504 +         )                                                        │
  │ 505 +                                                                  │
  │ 506 +     # Update statistics                                          │
  │ 507 +     new_ops = state.operations.copy()                            │
  │ 508 +     if record:                                                   │
  │ 509 +         new_ops.append(record)                                   │
  │ 510 +                                                                  │
  │ 511 +     new_state = CrusaderBridgeState(                             │
  │ 512 +         capabilities=state.capabilities,                         │
  │ 513 +         operations=new_ops,                                      │
  │ 514 +         exhaustion_attempts=state.exhaustion_attempts,           │
  │ 515 +         total_operations=state.total_operations + 1,             │
  │ 516 +         authorized_operations=state.authorized_operations + (1 … │
  │ 517 +         denied_operations=state.denied_operations + (0 if statu… │
  │ 518 +     )                                                            │
  │ 519 +                                                                  │
  │ 520 +     return new_state, status, record, combined_proof             │
  │ 521 +                                                                  │
  │ 522 +                                                                  │
  │ 523 + def get_ethical_audit_log(                                       │

  │ 574 +     def test_can_apply_force(self):                              │
  │ 575 +         """Test force level authorization."""                    │
  │ 576 +         cap = CrusaderCap(                                       │
  │ 577 +             holder_id="user1",                                   │
  │ 578 +             permissions=frozenset([Permission.EXECUTE]),         │
  │ 579 +             delegator="root",                                    │
  │ 580 +             just_cause="Test",                                   │
  │ 581 +             legitimate_authority="admin",                        │
  │ 582 +             max_force_level=Fraction(5, 10),  # 0.5 max          │
  │ 583 +             max_affected_resources=Fraction(100),                │
  │ 584 +             requires_exhaustion_attempts=False,                  │
  │ 585 +             exhaustion_attempts_required=0                       │
  │ 586 +         )                                                        │
